ii).astype(np.float32) / T\\n\\n        edges = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_bins + 1)\\n        edges[-1] += 1e-6\\n\\n        for b in range(n_bins):\\n            mask = (dists >= edges[b]) & (dists < edges[b + 1])\\n            valid = np.where(mask)[0]\\n            if len(valid) == 0:\\n                continue\\n            n = min(pairs_per_bin, len(valid))\\n            chosen = rng.choice(valid, size=n, replace=False)\\n            for c in chosen:\\n                all_pairs.append((pos, int(ii[c]), int(jj[c]), float(dists[c])))\\n\\n    return all_pairs\\n\\n\\ndef build_pair_tensors(feats, pairs):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build |h(t_j) - h(t_i)| inputs and distance targets.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    N = len(pairs)\\n    D = feats[0].shape[-1]\\n    X = np.empty((N, D), dtype=np.float32)\\n    y = np.empty(N, dtype=np.float32)\\n    for n, (pos, ti, tj, dist) in enumerate(pairs):\\n        X[n] = np.abs(feats[pos][tj] - feats[pos][ti])\\n        y[n] = dist\\n    return torch.from_numpy(X), torch.from_numpy(y)\\n\\n\\ndef build_oracle_tensors(pairs):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Oracle: input = scalar distance (same as target).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    X = torch.tensor([[p[3]] for p in pairs], dtype=torch.float32)\\n    y = torch.tensor([p[3] for p in pairs], dtype=torch.float32)\\n    return X, y\\n\\n\\ndef sample_cross_trajectory_pairs(traj_metas, indices, n_bins, pairs_per_bin, seed):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Cross-trajectory pair sampling (Control B negative control).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    valid = [(pos, idx) for pos, idx in enumerate(indices)\\n             if traj_metas[idx][\\\"T\\\"] >= 2]\\n    if len(valid) < 2:\\n        return []\\n\\n    n_v = len(valid)\\n    target_total = n_v * n_bins * pairs_per_bin\\n    n_cand = target_total * 5\\n\\n    Ts = np.array([traj_metas[valid[i][1]][\\\"T\\\"] for i in range(n_v)])\\n    poss = np.array([valid[i][0] for i in range(n_v)])\\n\\n    a_idx = rng.randint(n_v, size=n_cand)\\n    offsets = rng.randint(1, n_v, size=n_cand)\\n    b_idx = (a_idx + offsets) % n_v\\n\\n    t_a = (rng.random(n_cand) * Ts[a_idx]).astype(int)\\n    t_b = (rng.random(n_cand) * Ts[b_idx]).astype(int)\\n    norm_dist = np.abs(t_b / Ts[b_idx] - t_a / Ts[a_idx])\\n\\n    edges = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_bins + 1)\\n    edges[-1] += 1e-6\\n\\n    all_pairs = []\\n    per_bin = target_total // n_bins\\n    for bi in range(n_bins):\\n        mask = np.where((norm_dist >= edges[bi]) & (norm_dist < edges[bi + 1]))[0]\\n        if len(mask) == 0:\\n            continue\\n        n = min(per_bin, len(mask))\\n        chosen = rng.choice(mask, size=n, replace=False)\\n        for c in chosen:\\n            all_pairs.append((\\n                int(poss[a_idx[c]]), int(t_a[c]),\\n                int(poss[b_idx[c]]), int(t_b[c]),\\n                float(norm_dist[c]),\\n            ))\\n\\n    return all_pairs\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

feats[pos][ti])\\n        y[n] = dist\\n    return torch.from_numpy(X), torch.from_numpy(y)\\n\\n\\ndef build_oracle_tensors(pairs):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Oracle: input = scalar distance (same as target).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    X = torch.tensor([[p[3]] for p in pairs], dtype=torch.float32)\\n    y = torch.tensor([p[3] for p in pairs], dtype=torch.float32)\\n    return X, y\\n\\n\\ndef sample_cross_trajectory_pairs(traj_metas, indices, n_bins, pairs_per_bin, seed):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Cross-trajectory pair sampling (Control B negative control).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    valid = [(pos, idx) for pos, idx in enumerate(indices)\\n             if traj_metas[idx][\\\"T\\\"] >= 2]\\n    if len(valid) < 2:\\n        return []\\n\\n    n_v = len(valid)\\n    target_total = n_v * n_bins * pairs_per_bin\\n    n_cand = target_total * 5\\n\\n    Ts = np.array([traj_metas[valid[i][1]][\\\"T\\\"] for i in range(n_v)])\\n    poss = np.array([valid[i][0] for i in range(n_v)])\\n\\n    a_idx = rng.randint(n_v, size=n_cand)\\n    offsets = rng.randint(1, n_v, size=n_cand)\\n    b_idx = (a_idx + offsets) % n_v\\n\\n    t_a = (rng.random(n_cand) * Ts[a_idx]).astype(int)\\n    t_b = (rng.random(n_cand) * Ts[b_idx]).astype(int)\\n    norm_dist = np.abs(t_b / Ts[b_idx] - t_a / Ts[a_idx])\\n\\n    edges = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_bins + 1)\\n    edges[-1] += 1e-6\\n\\n    all_pairs = []\\n    per_bin = target_total // n_bins\\n    for bi in range(n_bins):\\n        mask = np.where((norm_dist >= edges[bi]) & (norm_dist < edges[bi + 1]))[0]\\n        if len(mask) == 0:\\n            continue\\n        n = min(per_bin, len(mask))\\n        chosen = rng.choice(mask, size=n, replace=False)\\n        for c in chosen:\\n            all_pairs.append((\\n                int(poss[a_idx[c]]), int(t_a[c]),\\n                int(poss[b_idx[c]]), int(t_b[c]),\\n                float(norm_dist[c]),\\n            ))\\n\\n    return all_pairs\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"
    }
  }

Add ActionMLPProbe class after MLPProbe\naction_probe_class = '''\n\nclass ActionMLPProbe(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, in_dim, out_dim=7, hidden_dim=256):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.Linear(in_dim, hidden_dim),\n            nn.ReLU(),\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, out_dim),\n        )\n\n    def forward(self, x):\n        return self.net(x)\n\n'''\n\ncode = code.replace(\n    'class MultiLabelProbe(nn.Module):',\n    action_probe_class + 'class MultiLabelProbe(nn.Module):'\n)\n\n# 4. 1)\n    train_ids, val_ids = split_by_task(traj_metas)\n\n    print(f\"Features: {len(traj_metas)} trajs, {n_layers} layers, dim={hidden_dim}\")\n    print(f\"Split: {len(train_ids)} train / {len(val_ids)} val\")\n\n    train_actions = load_actions(traj_metas, train_ids, args.data_dir)\n    val_actions = load_actions(traj_metas, val_ids, args.data_dir)\n\n    def build_shifted(feats, acts, ids_list):\n        Xs, Ys, task_ids = [], [], []\n        for i, (feat, act) in enumerate(zip(feats, acts)):\n            T = min(feat.shape[0], act.shape[0])\n            if T < 2:\n                continue\n            Xs.append(torch.from_numpy(feat[:T - 1]))\n            Ys.append(torch.from_numpy(act[1:T]))\n            task_ids.extend([traj_metas[ids_list[i]][\"task\"]] * (T - 1))\n        return torch.cat(Xs), torch.cat(Ys), task_ids\n\n    results = {}\n    for li, layer in enumerate(layers):\n        print(f\"\\\\n[Layer {layer}] ({li+1}/{len(layers)})\", flush=True)\n        t0 = time.time()\n\n        train_feats = load_layer_features(traj_metas, train_ids, layer)\n        val_feats = load_layer_features(traj_metas, val_ids, layer)\n\n        X_tr, y_tr_raw, _ = build_shifted(train_feats, train_actions, train_ids)\n        X_val, y_val_raw, val_tasks = build_shifted(val_feats, val_actions, val_ids)\n        del train_feats, val_feats\n\n        y_mean = y_tr_raw.mean(dim=0)\n        y_std = y_tr_raw.std(dim=0).clamp(min=1e-8)\n        y_tr = (y_tr_raw - y_mean) / y_std\n        y_val = (y_val_raw - y_mean) / y_std\n\n        print(f\"  Samples: {len(X_tr)} train / {len(X_val)} val\")\n\n        model = ActionMLPProbe(hidden_dim).to(device)\n        opt = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=args.lr)\n        crit = nn.MSELoss()\n\n        X_tr_d, y_tr_d = X_tr.to(device), y_tr.to(device)\n        X_val_d, y_val_d = X_val.to(device), y_val.to(device)\n\n        best_loss, best_state, wait, final_ep = float(\"inf\"), None, 0, 0\n        for ep in range(args.epochs):\n            model.train()\n            perm = torch.randperm(len(X_tr_d), device=device)\n            for s in range(0, len(X_tr_d), args.batch_size):\n                idx = perm[s : s + args.batch_size]\n                loss = crit(model(X_tr_d[idx]), y_tr_d[idx])\n                opt.zero_grad()\n                loss.backward()\n                opt.step()\n\n            model.eval()\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                vl = crit(model(X_val_d), y_val_d).item()\n\n            if vl < best_loss:\n                best_loss = vl\n                best_state = {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}\n                wait = 0\n            else:\n                wait += 1\n                if wait >= args.patience:\n                    final_ep = ep + 1\n                    break\n            final_ep = ep + 1\n\n        model.load_state_dict(best_state)\n        model.eval()\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            pred_std = model(X_val_d).cpu()\n\n        pred_raw = pred_std * y_std + y_mean\n        y_val_np = y_val_raw.numpy()\n        pred_np = pred_raw.numpy()\n\n        dim_names = [\"dx\", \"dy\", \"dz\", \"drx\", \"dry\", \"drz\", \"gripper\"]\n        per_dim_r2 = {}\n        for d in range(7):\n            per_dim_r2[dim_names[d]] = float(r2_score(y_val_np[:, d], pred_np[:, d]))\n\n        overall_r2 = float(r2_score(y_val_np.flatten(), pred_np.flatten()))\n        raw_mse = float(np.mean((y_val_np - pred_np) ** 2))\n\n        grip_pred = (pred_np[:, 6] > 0).astype(float)\n        grip_true = (y_val_np[:, 6] > 0).astype(float)\n        grip_acc = float(np.mean(grip_pred == grip_true))\n\n        val_tasks_arr = np.array(val_tasks)\n        unique_tasks = sorted(set(val_tasks))\n        per_task_r2 = {}\n        for task in unique_tasks:\n            mask = val_tasks_arr == task\n            if mask.sum() >= 2:\n                per_task_r2[task] = float(r2_score(\n                    y_val_np[mask].flatten(), pred_np[mask].flatten()))\n            else:\n                per_task_r2[task] = float(\"nan\")\n\n        valid_r2s = [v for v in per_task_r2.values() if not np.isnan(v)]\n        per_task_mean_r2 = float(np.mean(valid_r2s)) if valid_r2s else 0.0\n\n        m = dict(\n            layer=layer,\n            per_dim_r2=per_dim_r2,\n            overall_r2=overall_r2,\n            per_task_r2=per_task_r2,\n            per_task_mean_r2=per_task_mean_r2,\n            gripper_accuracy=grip_acc,\n            raw_mse=raw_mse,\n            val_loss=best_loss,\n            epochs_trained=final_ep,\n        )\n        results[layer] = m\n        del X_tr, y_tr, X_val, y_val, X_tr_d, y_tr_d, X_val_d, y_val_d\n\n        print(f\"  R2={overall_r2:.4f}  mean_task_R2={per_task_mean_r2:.4f}  \"\n              f\"grip_acc={grip_acc:.4f}  ep={final_ep}  {time.time()-t0:.1f}s\")\n\n    return results\n\n\n'''\n\ncode = code.replace(\n    'def run_temporal_distance(args, device):',\n    action_code + 'def run_temporal_distance(args, device):'\n)\n\n# 5. Add action_prediction to save_results\ncode = code.replace(\n    '    if probe_type in (\"temporal_distance\", \"oracle\"):\\n'\n    '        fields = [\"layer\", \"spearman_rho\", \"per_task_mean_rho\", \"spearman_p\",\\n'\n    '                   \"r2\", \"mae\", \"val_loss\", \"epochs_trained\"]\\n'\n    '    else:\\n'\n    '        fields = [\"layer\", \"macro_auc\", \"val_loss\", \"epochs_trained\"]',\n    \n    '    if probe_type in (\"temporal_distance\", \"oracle\"):\\n'\n    '        fields = [\"layer\", \"spearman_rho\", \"per_task_mean_rho\", \"spearman_p\",\\n'\n    '                   \"r2\", \"mae\", \"val_loss\", \"epochs_trained\"]\\n'\n    '    elif probe_type == \"action_prediction\":\\n'\n    '        fields = [\"layer\", \"overall_r2\", \"per_task_mean_r2\",\\n'\n    '                   \"gripper_accuracy\", \"raw_mse\", \"val_loss\", \"epochs_trained\"]\\n'\n    '    else:\\n'\n    '        fields = [\"layer\", \"macro_auc\", \"val_loss\", \"epochs_trained\"]'\n)\n\n# 6. Add action_prediction summary in save_results (after temporal_distance summary block)\ncode = code.replace(\n    '    if probe_type == \"temporal_distance\" and results:\\n'\n    '        # D002: best_layer selected by per-task mean ρ (not pooled ρ)\\n'\n    '        best = max(results, key=lambda k: results[k][\"per_task_mean_rho\"])\\n'\n    '        summary[\"best_layer\"] = best\\n'\n    '        summary[\"best_rho\"] = results[best][\"per_task_mean_rho\"]\\n'\n    '        summary[\"best_pooled_rho\"] = results[best][\"spearman_rho\"]',\n    \n    '    if probe_type == \"temporal_distance\" and results:\\n'\n    '        # D002: best_layer selected by per-task mean ρ (not pooled ρ)\\n'\n    '        best = max(results, key=lambda k: results[k][\"per_task_mean_rho\"])\\n'\n    '        summary[\"best_layer\"] = best\\n'\n    '        summary[\"best_rho\"] = results[best][\"per_task_mean_rho\"]\\n'\n    '        summary[\"best_pooled_rho\"] = results[best][\"spearman_rho\"]\\n'\n    '\\n'\n    '    if probe_type == \"action_prediction\" and results:\\n'\n    '        best = max(results, key=lambda k: results[k][\"per_task_mean_r2\"])\\n'\n    '        summary[\"best_layer\"] = best\\n'\n    '        summary[\"best_r2\"] = results[best][\"per_task_mean_r2\"]\\n'\n    '        summary[\"best_overall_r2\"] = results[best][\"overall_r2\"]'\n)\n\n# 7. Add action_prediction to runners dict in main()\ncode = code.replace(\n    '    runners = dict(\\n'\n    '        temporal_distance=run_temporal_distance,\\n'\n    '        oracle=run_oracle,\\n'\n    '        subtask_predicate=run_subtask_predicate,\\n'\n    '    )',\n    \n    '    runners = dict(\\n'\n    '        temporal_distance=run_temporal_distance,\\n'\n    '        oracle=run_oracle,\\n'\n    '        subtask_predicate=run_subtask_predicate,\\n'\n    '        action_prediction=run_action_prediction,\\n'\n    '    )'\n)\n\nwith open(\"/root/vla-ssd/scripts/train_probes.py\", \"w\") as f:\n    f.write(code)\n\nprint(\"Patch applied successfully\")\nPYEOF\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 /tmp/patch_action_probe.py"

_source line 97_

全部验证通过。实现完成，结果如下：

**Layer 4 (trained) 测试结果：**
- Overall R² = 0.536, Per-task mean R² = 0.543
- Gripper accuracy = 85.5%
- Per-dim R²: dx=0.47, dy=0.61, dz=0.35, drx=0.37, dry=0.18, drz=0.63, gripper=0.55
- Raw MSE = 0.090

**代码修改（`/root/vla-ssd/scripts/train_probes.py`）：**
1. `ActionMLPProbe` class — 7-dim 输出的 MLP
2. `load_actions()` — 从 LIBERO HDF5 加载 action，自动处理 `demo_X`/`traj_X` key 映射
3. `run_action_prediction()` — 完整 runner：shifted pairs (h_t→a_{t+1})、per-dim z-score standardization、训练+early stopping、per-dim R²/overall R²/per-task R²/gripper accuracy/raw MSE
4. `parse_args` 加了 `action_prediction` choice 和 `--data_dir` 参数
5. `save_results` 加了 action_prediction 的 CSV fields 和 summary best_layer 选择
6. `main` runners dict 加了 `action_prediction`

备份在 `train_probes.py.bak3`。
